# Graph Report - ferros-grynn  (2026-09-05)

## Corpus Check
- 2217 files · ~2,493,037 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 48611 nodes · 124650 edges · 1835 communities (1599 shown, 236 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2536 edges (avg confidence: 0.8)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `713d90c6`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
